# Graph Report - Teplo-all-for-business  (2026-10-25)

## Corpus Check
- 1336 files · ~8,871,899 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 28103 nodes · 83195 edges · 1186 communities (1027 shown, 159 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 79% EXTRACTED · 21% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 17186 edges (avg confidence: 0.63)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `a2bdf91a`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
